SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2，读取代码：\n   ```\n   cat /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/src/w2_domain_bias.py\n   ```\n   重点关注 seed group 分配逻辑（搜索 `sample_id`、`group`、`seed`、`logo` 关键词）。\n\n2. 检查 features.jsonl 的实际结构：\n   ```\n   head -5 /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/pythia_nucleus095/features.jsonl\n   ```\n   或其他路径下的特征文件。确认是否有 `sample_id` 字段。\n\n3. 检查数据生成流程：\n   - 查看数据生成脚本（可能在 `src/` 下），确认每个 depth 的 passage 是否从相同种子文章生成\n   - 如果是无条件生成（BOS token），则不存在 \"同一 seed 文章\" 的概念，sequential assignment 也无法建立跨 depth 对应关系\n\n4. 判断结论：\n   - **如果** 数据是无条件生成（无 seed 文章对应关系）：sequential ID 实际上是随机分组（因为生成顺序与内容无关），LOGO 测试的是 \"不同随机子集\" 之间的稳定性，仍然有效但含义不同——应在论文中澄清\n   - **如果** 数据有 seed 文章对应关系且行序匹配：逻辑正确\n   - **如果** 数据有 seed 文章对应关系但行序不匹配：LOGO 结果不可靠，需修复\n\n## 产出\n在 result 中报告：\n1. seed group 分配的具体代码逻辑\n2. features.jsonl 的实际字段结构\n3. 数据生成是否为无条件生成\n4. 结论：LOGO 逻辑是否成立，是否需要修复\n5. 如果需要修复，建议方案"
}

&lt;project_context>
# 项目: recursive_gen_depth_est

## 运行环境
- 服务器名: gpu-6000pro-2
- SSH 连接命令: ssh connect.westb.seetacloud.com  # 必须用此命令，~/.ssh/config 自动应用密钥和跳板机，禁止用裸 IP
- 项目代码根目录: /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est

## ⚠️ 活跃约束（必须遵守）

- **C001**: 提交同系列实验（如 DCOL v5/v6）前必须先 read_registry 检查该系列所有历史实验的失败原因和参数约束，确认新 spec 不重复已知错误（原因：DCOL 系列 4 次失败，v4 重复了 v1 完全相同的错误（margin_lr=1e-3 导致 embedding collapse）。Worker 写 spec 时未查阅同系列历史教训。）
- **C002**: 提交 DCOL 系列新实验前必须先 read_registry 检查同系列所有历史实验的失败原因和参数约束（原因：DCOL v4 重复了 v1 完全相同的错误（margin_lr=1e-3 导致 embedding collapse），系列已连续 4 次失败。Worker 未查阅历史教训直接提交。）


## 环境搭建
连接服务器后先调 `detect_image` 检测是否为预置镜像。如果匹配，跳过基础环境搭建，只装项目特有依赖。已预装的包不要重复安装。

## 输出风格
回复简洁直接。只输出执行结果和必要信息，不要解释过程、不要客套、不要给未被要求的建议。
&lt;/project_context>
低优先级验证任务：检查 w2_domain_bias.py 中 seed group 分配逻辑的正确性。

## 背景
W2 domain bias 实验使用 seed-stratified Leave-One-Group-Out (LOGO) 验证。Reviewer 质疑：当 features.jsonl 中不存在 `sample_id` 字段时，代码按 per-depth 顺序分配 sequential ID 作为 seed group。如果行序不对应相同种子文章，则 LOGO 的 "同一 seed 跨 depth" 假设不成立。

## 检查步骤

1. SSH 到 gpu-6000pro-2，读取代码：
   ```
   cat /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/src/w2_domain_bias.py
   ```
   重点关注 seed group 分配逻辑（搜索 `sample_id`、`group`、`seed`、`logo` 关键词）。

2. 检查 features.jsonl 的实际结构：
   ```
   head -5 /root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/pythia_nucleus095/features.jsonl
   ```
   或其他路径下的特征文件。确认是否有 `sample_id` 字段。

3. 检查数据生成流程：
   - 查看数据生成脚本（可能在 `src/` 下），确认每个 depth 的 passage 是否从相同种子文章生成
   - 如果是无条件生成（BOS token），则不存在 "同一 seed 文章" 的概念，sequential assignment 也无法建立跨 depth 对应关系

4. 判断结论：
   - **如果** 数据是无条件生成（无 seed 文章对应关系）：sequential ID 实际上是随机分组（因为生成顺序与内容无关），LOGO 测试的是 "不同随机子集" 之间的稳定性，仍然有效但含义不同——应在论文中澄清
   - **如果** 数据有 seed 文章对应关系且行序匹配：逻辑正确
   - **如果** 数据有 seed 文章对应关系但行序不匹配：LOGO 结果不可靠，需修复

## 产出
在 result 中报告：
1. seed group 分配的具体代码逻辑
2. features.jsonl 的实际字段结构
3. 数据生成是否为无条件生成
4. 结论：LOGO 逻辑是否成立，是否需要修复
5. 如果需要修复，建议方案

def get_seed_ids(df_syn):
    if "sample_id" in df_syn.columns:
        return df_syn["sample_id"].values.copy()
    seed_ids = np.zeros(len(df_syn), dtype=int)


```

When `sample_id` isn't available, the function iterates through each depth level and assigns sequential IDs within each group, so sample 0 at depth 1 corresponds to sample 0 at depth 2. Then `assign_groups` partitions these seed IDs into 10 groups, and `subexp_b` uses LOGO to split by group.

But I need to verify whether the sequential ordering within each depth actually maps to the same underlying seed article — let me look at the features.jsonl structure and how the data was generated.

<details><summary>Logged reasoning block</summary>

```text
All 9 feature files have `sample_id` field. The fallback sequential assignment logic is never triggered.

Now I need to understand what `sample_id` means in the data generation context. Let me check the generation scripts to understand whether data is seed-based (Wikipedia seed articles) or unconditional generation.

Wait, I notice something interesting: many feature files share the exact same depth=0 row data (same mean_ppl, var_ppl values). For example, pythia_nucleus095 and pythia_temp09 and olmo files share exactly the same depth=0, sample_id=0 row. 

This makes sense — depth 0 represents the original Wikipedia seed article, so it should be identical across different decoding strategies and models since they're all starting from the same source. But I'm seeing that pythia_nucleus095, gpt2xl, and olmo all have the same depth=0 mean_ppl value of 1607.15, while gpt2xl_temp09 shows a different value of 9474.13, which suggests the seed data might vary by model or there's something else going on with how the data was generated.

Looking at the lexical features like type_token_ratio, I'm noticing they differ slightly between models even at depth 0 — features_all.jsonl shows 0.570 while gpt2xl_temp09 shows 0.5854 — which is odd if they're supposed to be evaluating the same seed text.

Actually, examining the data more carefully, most models (pythia_nucleus095, gpt2xl, olmo, pythia_temp09) share the same type_token_ratio of 0.570281 at depth 0, but gpt2xl_temp09 diverges with 0.585366, while olmo_temp09 aligns back with the first group. This inconsistency is puzzling, but I should focus on the main issue: whether sample_id actually links samples consistently across different cells.

Let me check the data generation code to see how texts are created and how sample_id gets assigned.
```

{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\"\\\"\\\"用 LoRA 微调后的模型无条件生成下一代文本。\\n\\nseed_id 为独立递增编号（0..num_samples-1），不与上一代数据对应。\\nmax_new_tokens 默认 512，与 depth-0 的 token 范围（256-512）一致，\\n避免生成文本系统性偏短导致分类器学到长度 confound。\\n\\\"\\\"\\\"\\n\\nimport argparse\\nimport json\\nimport os\\nimport torch\\nfrom transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM, AutoTokenizer\\nfrom peft import PeftModel\\n\\n\\ndef parse_args():\\n    p = argparse.ArgumentParser(description=\\\"Generate next-depth text with LoRA model\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--adapter_dir\\\", required=True, help=\\\"LoRA adapter path\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--output_path\\\", required=True, help=\\\"Output jsonl path\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model_name\\\", default=\\\"EleutherAI/pythia-1.4b\\\", help=\\\"Base model name\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--model_cache\\\", default=\\\"/root/autodl-tmp/models/pythia-1.4b\\\", help=\\\"Model cache dir\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--target_depth\\\", type=int, required=True, help=\\\"Target depth (1, 2, 3)\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--num_samples\\\", type=int, default=5000, help=\\\"Number of samples to generate\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--max_new_tokens\\\", type=int, default=512)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--temperature\\\", type=float, default=1.0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--top_p\\\", type=float, default=0.95)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--top_k\\\", type=int, default=50)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--decoding_strategy\\\", default=\\\"nucleus\\\",\\n                   choices=[\\\"nucleus\\\", \\\"topk\\\", \\\"temp_only\\\"],\\n                   help=\\\"Decoding strategy: nucleus (top_p), topk (top_k), temp_only\\\")\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--batch_size\\\", type=int, default=16)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--gpu\\\", type=int, default=0)\\n    p.add_argument(\\\"--seed\\\", type=int, default=42)\\n    return p.parse_args()\\n\\n\\ndef main():\\n    args = parse_args()\\n\\n    if \\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\" not in os.environ:\\n        os.environ[\\\"CUDA_VISIBLE_DEVICES\\\"] = str(args.gpu)\\n    device = torch.device(\\\"cuda:0\\\")\\n    torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading base model: {args.model_name}\\\")\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model_name,\\n        cache_dir=args.model_cache,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n    )\\n    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:\\n        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token\\n\\n    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model_name,\\n        cache_dir=args.model_cache,\\n        torch_dtype=torch.bfloat16,\\n        trust_remote_code=True,\\n    )\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading LoRA adapter: {args.adapter_dir}\\\")\\n    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.adapter_dir)\\n    model = model.to(device)\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    total = args.num_samples\\n    print(f\\\"Generating {total} samples at depth {args.target_depth}\\\")\\n\\n    bos_token_id = tokenizer.bos_token_id\\n    if bos_token_id is None:\\n        bos_token_id = tokenizer.eos_token_id\\n\\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output_path), exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    generated = 0\\n    with open(args.output_path, \\\"w\\\", encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\") as fout:\\n        for batch_start in range(0, total, args.batch_size):\\n            batch_end = min(batch_start + args.batch_size, total)\\n            cur_batch_size = batch_end - batch_start\\n\\n            input_ids = torch.tensor([[bos_token_id]] * cur_batch_size, device=device)\\n            attention_mask = torch.ones_like(input_ids)\\n\\n            gen_kwargs = {\\n                \\\"input_ids\\\": input_ids,\\n                \\\"attention_mask\\\": attention_mask,\\n                \\\"max_new_tokens\\\": args.max_new_tokens,\\n                \\\"do_sample\\\": True,\\n                \\\"pad_token_id\\\": tokenizer.pad_token_id,\\n            }\\n            if args.decoding_strategy == \\\"nucleus\\\":\\n                gen_kwargs[\\\"top_p\\\"] = args.top_p\\n                gen_kwargs[\\\"temperature\\\"] = args.temperature\\n            elif args.decoding_strategy == \\\"topk\\\":\\n                gen_kwargs[\\\"top_k\\\"] = args.top_k\\n                gen_kwargs[\\\"temperature\\\"] = args.temperature\\n            elif args.decoding_strategy == \\\"temp_only\\\":\\n                gen_kwargs[\\\"temperature\\\"] = args.temperature\\n\\n            with torch.no_grad():\\n                outputs = model.generate(**gen_kwargs)\\n\\n            for i, output in enumerate(outputs):\\n                text = tokenizer.decode(output[1:], skip_special_tokens=True)\\n                token_count = len(output) - 1\\n                record = {\\n                    \\\"text\\\": text,\\n                    \\\"depth\\\": args.target_depth,\\n                    \\\"seed_id\\\": batch_start + i,\\n                    \\\"token_count\\\": token_count,\\n                }\\n                fout.write(json.dumps(record, ensure_ascii=False) + \\\"\\\\n\\\")\\n\\n            generated += cur_batch_size\\n            if generated % (args.batch_size * 10) == 0 or generated == total:\\n                print(f\\\"  Generated {generated}/{total}\\\")\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Done. {total} samples saved to {args.output_path}\\\")\\n\\n\\nif __name__ == \\\"__main__\\\":\\n    main()\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 0.74}}"}

torch.manual_seed(args.seed)\\n\\n    print(f\\\"Loading base model: {args.model_name}\\\")\\n    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(\\n        args.model_name,\\n print(f\\\"Loading LoRA adapter: {args.adapter_dir}\\\")\\n    model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, args.adapter_dir)\\n    model = model.to(device)\\n    model.eval()\\n\\n    total = tokenizer.eos_token_id\\n\\n    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(args.output_path), exist_ok=True)\\n\\n    generated = 0\\n    with open(args.output_path, \\\"w\\\", encoding=\\\"utf-8\\\") as fout:\\n        for batch_start in range(0, total, args.batch_size):\\n            batch_end = min(batch_start + args.batch_size, total)\\n            cur_batch_size = batch_end - batch_start\\n\\n            input_ids = torch.tensor([[bos_token_id]] * cur_batch_size, device=device)\\n            attention_mask = torch.ones_like(input_ids)\\n\\n            gen_kwargs = {\\n                \\\"input_ids\\\": input_ids,\\n                \\\"attention_mask\\\": attention_mask,\\n                \\\"max_new_tokens\\\": args.max_new_tokens,\\n                \\\"do_sample\\\": True,\\n with torch.no_grad():\\n                outputs = model.generate(**gen_kwargs)\\n\\n            for i, output in enumerate(outputs):\\n                text = tokenizer.decode(output[1:], This is problematic for LOGO validation because I'm leaving out groups that are almost entirely from a single depth, when the whole point is to leave out groups that represent the full range of depths. I should double-check how sample_id is actually being assigned in the extraction code.

Confirmed! The `sample_id` in features_all.jsonl is a GLOBAL sequential counter:
- Depth 0: sample_id 0..4999 (5000 samples)
- Depth 1: sample_id 5000..9999 (5000 samples)
- (Presumably) Depth 2: sample_id 10000..14999
- (Presumably) Depth 3: sample_id 15000..19999

Total: 20000 rows, 5000 per depth.

Now let's trace through the LOGO logic in `subexp_b`:


The function filters to depths 1-3, then extracts the sample_id values (5000..19999) as seed IDs and passes them to `assign_groups` to partition them into groups.

Now I'm seeing how the grouping works — it divides the 15000 unique seed IDs into 10 groups of 1500 each by mapping each seed's sorted index to a group number, so seeds 5000-6499 go to group 0, 6500-7999 to group 1, and so on through group 9. I'm seeing a critical issue with how LOGO is stratifying the data—it's removing entire groups that are heavily skewed toward specific depths, which creates severe class imbalance in the training folds. For instance, dropping group 0 eliminates 1500 depth-1 samples while leaving depth-2 and depth-3 untouched, leaving the fold with only 3500 depth-1 samples against 5000 each of the other depths. Let me look more carefully at what subexp_b is actually doing...

Examining the function logic...

The setup uses LeaveOneGroupOut to create 10 folds, where each fold leaves out one group entirely for testing. In fold 0, the test set would be those 1500 depth-1 samples from group 0, while the classifier trains on groups 1-9 which still contain all three depths represented. The model should theoretically perform reasonably well since it's seen the full depth distribution during training, though the test accuracy on that particular fold would reflect how well it generalizes to depth-1 specifically.

But I'm realizing this isn't actually implementing seed-stratified LOGO the way it was intended. The goal should be grouping all samples from the same seed article together across all depths, then leaving out entire seed groups to test whether the model generalizes to unseen seed articles. Since the sample IDs are global rather than per-depth, the groups don't properly span across depth boundaries—the LOGO is essentially doing a contiguous block split that happens to correlate with depth instead.

That said, I should consider whether this actually undermines the results. The core question seed-stratified LOGO tries to answer is whether the classifier generalizes to unseen seed articles, and if there's no seed article confound in the data to begin with, then this implementation issue might not matter much in practice.

The real problem is that the groups aren't balanced across depths—groups 0-2 contain only depth-1 samples while groups 7-9 contain only depth-3 samples. This means each fold tests on a different depth composition, which could skew the reported accuracy. The more pressing question is whether this actually affects the conclusions I'm drawing from the W2 experiment.

If the generation is truly unconditional from the BOS token as the code suggests, then there's no seed text confound to worry about in the first place. That fundamentally changes what the LOGO result means—it's not testing whether depth is a domain artifact, but rather showing that a block-contiguous split happens to correlate with sample ordering within each depth. I need to verify whether the code is actually writing and using a `seed_id` field or if it's just generating from the previous depth unconditionally.

Looking at the actual implementation, the generation script writes a `seed_id` field but the feature extraction script ignores it entirely and creates its own global `sample_id` counter instead. So even if I tried to use the `seed_id` to trace back to original Wikipedia articles, it wouldn't matter because the features file has already lost that connection.

The only place where `seed_id` might actually correspond to something meaningful is in depth 0, where it would map to different Wikipedia articles, but depth 0 is filtered out of the subexperiment anyway. Let me think through whether the fallback approach of assigning sequential IDs per depth would have been better...

Checking fallback logic...

If we'd assigned seed IDs sequentially within each depth instead, we'd get the same 0-4999 range repeated across depths 1, 2, and 3. Then when grouping, we'd end up with 5000 unique seeds total, split into groups of 500, where each group pulls equally from all three depths — so group 0 would contain 1500 samples (500 from each depth), group 1 another 1500, and so on.

That per-depth sequential approach would actually be better for LOGO since it ensures balanced depth representation in each fold, even though neither method truly captures seed-article correspondence since the generation is unconditional.

The real issue is that `get_seed_ids` pulls global sequential IDs from features.jsonl (0 through 19999 across all depths), and `assign_groups` chunks these into 10 contiguous blocks, which creates heavy depth correlation in the groups. 段**: Has `sample_id` (global counter), `depth`, and 19 features. sample_id ranges: depth 0 = 0-4999, depth 1 = 5000-9999, etc.

3. **数据生成是否为无条件**: YES. `generate_next_depth.py` explicitly states "seed_id 为独立递增编号，不与上一代数据对应" and generates from BOS token unconditionally.

4. **LOGO 逻辑是否成立**: **有 bug**. 因为 sample_id 是全局递增的，groups 严重偏向特定 depth。LOGO fold 不是 depth-balanced 的。但因为生成本身是无条件的，所以 LOGO 的 "同一 seed 跨 depth" 假设本就不成立。

5. **修复建议**: 我有三个选项——删掉 features.jsonl 中的 sample_id 让 fallback 逻辑生效以确保 depth-balanced groups，改为 per-depth counter 重置 sample_id，或在 w2_domain_bias.py 中直接用 per-depth sequential assignment。无论选哪个，都需要在论文中澄清：由于生成是无条件的，LOGO 测试的是不同随机子集间的分类稳定性，而非跨 seed article 的泛化能力。

既然 LOGO groups 与 depth 相关，但分类器确实在预测 depth，那准确率会是什么样的呢？在 fold 0 中（排除全是 depth 1 的 group 0），测试集全是 depth 1，训练集包含所有 depth，随机森林应该能正确分类这些 depth-1 样本。但在 fold 7 中（排除全是 depth 3 的 group 7），测试集全是 depth 3，训练集缺少 depth 3 的数据...

而在 fold 3 中，测试集是混合的，包含 depth 1 和 depth 2。最终报告的 LOGO 准确率是各 fold 的加权平均，权重取决于每个 depth 在各 fold 中的出现频率，这导致 fold 间的方差比真正平衡的 LOGO 要高。所以这个 LOGO 结果在技术上作为"seed-stratified"实验是有问题的，但对整体结论的影响可能不是灾难性的——更像是一个设计不当的交叉验证，虽然仍然能得出某种结论。

Now I have a complete picture. Let me summarize.

Depth 0 is Wikipedia articles with `source: "wikipedia"` and `seed_id` per article.

Depth 1+ is generated unconditionally from BOS token by a LoRA-finetuned model. The docstring in generate_next_depth.py explicitly states: "seed_id 为独立递增编号（0..num_samples-1），不与上一代数据对应。"

Then `extract_features.py` creates a GLOBAL `sample_id` counter, overwriting/ignoring the per-depth `seed_id`.


In `w2_domain_bias.py`, `get_seed_ids` returns this global `sample_id`, and `assign_groups` partitions it into 10 contiguous blocks. Since the global counter increments monotonically across depths, the group assignments end up strongly correlated with depth—which is a bug that needs fixing.

_source line 85 · 2026-05-15T19:44:03.437Z_

完整分析完成。

## 结果

### 1. seed group 分配的具体代码逻辑

`get_seed_ids()` 直接返回 `sample_id` 列。`extract_features.py` 中 `sample_id` 是**全局递增计数器**（不是 per-depth），结果：
- depth 0: sample_id 0–4999
- depth 1: sample_id 5000–9999
- depth 2: sample_id 10000–14999
- depth 3: sample_id 15000–19999

`assign_groups()` 将 15000 个唯一 sample_id（depth 1–3）按顺序切 10 块，每块 1500 个。导致：
- Group 0–2: **纯 depth 1**
- Group 3, 6: depth 交界混合
- Group 7–9: **纯 depth 3**

### 2. features.jsonl 字段

`{"depth": int, "sample_id": int(全局), mean_ppl, var_ppl, ...}` — 19 个统计特征。

### 3. 数据生成是无条件的

`generate_next_depth.py` 文档注释明确写道：**"seed_id 为独立递增编号（0..num_samples-1），不与上一代数据对应。"** 生成从 BOS token 开始，无 seed 文章输入。depth 0 是 Wikipedia 原文，depth 1+ 是 LoRA 模型无条件生成。不存在跨 depth 的 seed 文章对应关系。

### 4. 结论：LOGO 逻辑有 bug

**有两层问题：**

- **Bug（技术层）**：`sample_id` 是全局计数器，LOGO groups 严重偏向特定 depth。留出 group 0 = 测试集全是 depth 1；留出 group 9 = 测试集全是 depth 3。这不是 seed-stratified LOGO，而是 depth-correlated block split。
- **语义层**：由于生成是无条件的，"同一 seed 跨 depth"的假设本就不成立。即使修复了 bug，LOGO 测试的也是"不同随机子集间的分类稳定性"，不是"跨 seed article 的泛化"。

**结果可能碰巧还行**（因为 RF 在每个 fold 仍能见到所有 depth 的训练样本），但 fold 间 variance 会偏高，且含义与论文声称的不符。

### 5. 修复建议

**代码修复**（最小改动，改 `get_seed_ids`）：

```python
def get_seed_ids(df_syn):
    # 始终使用 per-depth sequential assignment
    # 因为生成是无条件的，sample_id 无跨 depth 语义
    seed_ids = np.zeros(len(df_syn), dtype=int)
    for d in sorted(df_syn["depth"].unique()):
        mask = (df_syn["depth"] == d).values
        seed_ids[mask] = np.arange(mask.sum())
    return seed_ids
```

这样每个 depth 内 seed_id 0–4999，groups 会 depth-balanced（每组在每个 depth 各有 ~500 样本）。

**论文需澄清**：由于数据是无条件生成的，LOGO 验证的是"分类器在不同随机子集间的稳定性"，而非"跨 seed domain 的泛化能力"。建议将 "seed-stratified LOGO" 改为 "block-stratified LOGO" 或类似表述。
